# velvet-icon

**[Browse all icons and try the live playground →](https://poojagohel.github.io/velvet-icon/)**

A framework-agnostic, **zero runtime dependency** SVG icon library for TypeScript and JavaScript. Icons are exposed as ready-to-use SVG strings that you can size, recolor, and re-stroke on the fly.

- Zero runtime dependencies — `typescript` is the only devDependency
- Ships ESM, CommonJS, and full TypeScript type definitions
- Works anywhere: plain JS/TS, React, Vue, Svelte, server-side rendering
- Fully typed `IconName` union — invalid icon names are caught at compile time, with a runtime guard as a backstop
- Tree-shakeable — import only the icons you use and ship ~0.1 KB per icon

## Install

```bash
npm install velvet-icon
```

## Usage (vanilla JS/TS)

```ts
import { getIcon } from "velvet-icon";

const svg = getIcon("home", { size: 32, color: "#4f46e5", strokeWidth: 1.5 });

document.querySelector("#nav-home")!.innerHTML = svg;
```

`getIcon` returns a plain string, so it works anywhere you can insert HTML/SVG markup — including on the server.

```ts
import { getIcon, icons, type IconName } from "velvet-icon";

// All defaults: 24x24, currentColor stroke, stroke-width 2
getIcon("search");

// Enumerate every available icon name
const allNames = Object.keys(icons) as IconName[];
```

## Smallest bundles: import only the icons you use

Every icon is also a named export with an `Icon` suffix (`home` → `homeIcon`). Pair them with
`renderIcon` and your bundler drops every icon you don't import:

```ts
import { renderIcon, homeIcon, searchIcon } from "velvet-icon";

renderIcon(homeIcon, { size: 32, color: "#4f46e5" });
renderIcon(searchIcon);
```

Measured with esbuild (minified + gzipped): one icon ≈ 0.3 KB including the renderer, ten icons ≈ 0.9 KB.
`getIcon(name)` looks icons up by string, so it pulls in the full registry (~400 KB gzipped) — use it
when you need dynamic names, such as an icon picker. Tree-shaking applies to the ESM build.

## Usage with React

```tsx
import { getIcon, type IconName, type IconOptions } from "velvet-icon";

interface IconProps extends IconOptions {
  name: IconName;
  className?: string;
}

export function Icon({ name, className, ...options }: IconProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={className}
      // getIcon() only ever renders the library's own bundled SVG markup, so this is safe.
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: getIcon(name, options) }}
    />
  );
}

// <Icon name="check" size={20} color="green" />
```

## API

### `getIcon(name, options?)`

Renders an icon as a self-contained `<svg>...</svg>` string.

| Option | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `size` | `number` | `24` | Width/height, in pixels |
| `color` | `string` | `"currentColor"` | Applied to `stroke` |
| `strokeWidth` | `number` | `2` | Applied to `stroke-width` |

Throws an `Error` if `name` is not a registered icon.

### `renderIcon(icon, options?)`

Renders an icon definition such as `homeIcon` with the same options as `getIcon`. It doesn't touch
the by-name registry, so it keeps bundles tree-shaken.

### `<name>Icon` exports

One `IconDefinition` per icon, for example `homeIcon`, `arrowRightIcon`, `packageIcon`.

### `icons`

The raw icon registry: a const object mapping icon name → `{ viewBox, path }`.

### `IconName`

A union type of every valid icon name, derived directly from `icons`.

## Development

```bash
npm run build:icons   # parse src/icons/*.svg -> src/generated/icons.ts
npm run build         # build:icons, then compile ESM + CJS + type declarations to dist/
npm test              # run the node:test suite
```
